# Bench results archive index (ozarchy `~/bench-results-matched`)

Made 2026-10-07 (main checkout, branch main). The raw data lives **on ozarchy only**, in
`/home/oz/bench-results-matched/`; none of it is in git. This archive is the docs side of a clean-up: it records where
each campaign's results are written up, adds a summary file for every campaign the docs do not (fully) cover, and lists the
heavy raw files that can be deleted once these docs are committed (`DELETABLE.md`). Nothing under
`~/bench-results-matched` was deleted, moved or modified to make this archive.

**Updated 2026-10-08 (s29).** Added 60 dirs written since the snapshot and inventoried the 4 dirs skipped at archive time (their rows changed from S to R), sizes and dates taken 2026-10-08 ~17:45, 13.82 GiB in the 64 dirs: campaigns `ozarchy-p2s0b-*` (section 25 of the ozarchy doc), `ozarchy-p2s0r-*` (section 26), `ozarchy-p2s0x-*` (section 27), `ozarchy-p2s0y-*` (section 28) with their `-build`, `-stage`, `-tools`, `-smoke-*`, `ozarchy-p2s0b-hasher` and `ozarchy-p2s0b-spawn` dirs; pre-merge suite runs `presuite-105a6e28`, `presuite-b26bd3b3`, `presuite-c2-set-holder`, `presuite-item6-p2s0`; `read-gas-stall`; and `presuite-4164382d`, `presuite-76eb081c`, `presuite-ae767806`, `read-precompile-gas-ac`. After the c2h campaign finished (2026-10-08 ~19:15) the 18 `ozarchy-c2h*` dirs were added too (4.28 GiB: 14 cells, `-build`, `c2h2-build`, `-stage`, `-tools`; section 29 of the ozarchy doc), and their scripts copied to `tools/`. `presuite-c2-set-holder` is written up on branch `perf/c2-set-holder` (`c051872b`, pushed to origin), not on this branch. By this update every file in the original `DELETABLE.md` lists had already been deleted on ozarchy, so the sizes in the older rows are archive-time sizes. Top-level files: now 648 (3.4 MiB), all kept. New cells are in `cells.md`, new deletable files in `DELETABLE.md`.

**Updated 2026-10-09 (p3s0).** After the p3s0 campaign finished (2026-10-09 07:53) the 7 `ozarchy-p3s0*` dirs were added (2.09 GiB: 5 cells, `-build`, `-stage`; section 34 of the ozarchy doc), sizes (apparent) and dates taken 2026-10-09 ~08:20; commit and node md5 from each cell's `summary.json`. `ozarchy-p3s0-campaign.sh` and `ozarchy-p3s0-build.sh` were copied to `tools/`. The cells are in `cells.md`, the deletable files in `DELETABLE.md` (block `p3s0`). The campaigns between c2h and p3s0 (`p21g`, `p2byid`, `p25g`, `p22g`) have rows here and in `cells.md` but no `DELETABLE.md` block yet.

**Updated 2026-10-09 (p3s1).** After the p3s1 campaign finished (2026-10-09 10:40) the 15 `ozarchy-p3s1*` dirs were added (1.33 GiB: 13 cells, `-build`, `-stage`; section 35 of the ozarchy doc), sizes (apparent) and dates taken 2026-10-09 ~10:47; commit and node md5 from each cell's `summary.json`. Arms A (`1b389700`) and C (`3efff0d6`) ran the staged binaries in `ozarchy-p3s0-stage/a` and `/b`; only B (`3aa516e0`) was built and staged in `ozarchy-p3s1-stage`. `ozarchy-p3s1-campaign.sh`, `ozarchy-p3s1-build.sh` and `ozarchy-p3s1-analysis.py` were copied to `tools/`. The cells are in `cells.md`, the deletable files in `DELETABLE.md` (block `p3s1`).

**Updated 2026-10-09 (bkm).** After the bkm campaign finished (2026-10-09 13:14) the 12 `ozarchy-bkm*` dirs were added (1.24 GiB: 10 cells, `-build`, `-stage`; section 36 of the ozarchy doc), sizes (apparent) and dates taken 2026-10-09 ~13:26; commit and node md5 from each cell's `summary.json`. **Updated 2026-10-10 (acc, acc2).** After the compaction tuning screen (`ozarchy-acc`, 13:19-14:27) and its confirm (`ozarchy-acc2`, 18:32-21:21) the 43 `ozarchy-acc*` dirs were added (2.43 GiB: 38 cells, `ozarchy-acc-build`, `-stage`, `-run`, `ozarchy-acc2-run`; sections 38 and 39 of the ozarchy doc), sizes (apparent) and dates taken 2026-10-10 ~21:30; commit and node md5 from each cell's `summary.json`. The `worktree @ commit` field is the harness (`9b7e29b2` tools); every cell but `ozarchy-acc-300m-c-r1` (old node `6a71ba5f` from `ozarchy-p3s0r-stage/m`) ran node `2ede76eb` from `perf/append-cf-compaction` `fa8b646f`, staged in `ozarchy-acc-stage/n`. Arms differ only by node-local env (sections 38-39). Every cell holds `rocksdb-LOG-val{0,1,2}.txt`, `codec-check-val*.txt` and (acc2) `lsm-val*.txt`; the 9 acc2 restart cells (`rs-*`) also `restart-metrics.json`, `crash.json` and `crash-restart-tail.log` (keep). The first acc2 launch stopped at `t-r1` on its trade-row check and was resumed at `t-r1` with `OVERWRITE=1`, so the first `t-r1` survives only as `ozarchy-acc2-run/campaign-run1.log`. `ozarchy-acc-campaign.sh`, `-build.sh`, `-codec-check.py`, `ozarchy-acc2-campaign.sh`, `-lsm.py`, `-restart.py`, `-tables.py` and the acc2 analyst scripts (`ozarchy-acc2-tools/`: `cells.py`, `thr.py`; they ran from `/tmp` on ozarchy) were copied to `tools/`. The cells are in `cells.md`, the deletable files in `DELETABLE.md` (block `acc`; the stage binaries are kept while the owner decides the node defaults).

* **Scope.** 215 directories at the top level of `~/bench-results-matched`, plus 518 small top-level files
  (campaign drivers `*-campaign.sh`, campaign logs and done markers, per-cell `*.campaign.log` / `*.cell.rc`, analysis
  `*.md` / `*.txt` / `*.py`, ~4 MB in total). The top-level files are small and are kept; several docs cite them by name.
* **Skipped (in use at archive time).** `read-precompile-gas-ac` (owner instruction) and every dir with a file modified in the
  last 60 minutes (`presuite-4164382d`, `presuite-76eb081c`, `presuite-ae767806`). They are listed below but not inventoried and not in `DELETABLE.md`. (s29: all 4 now inventoried, see the update note.)
* **Sizes** are the sum of file sizes (`du --apparent-size` style), taken at archive time. The inventoried dirs (all but the 4 skipped) hold 8.69 GiB.
* **Dates** are the file-mtime range (local date, ozarchy).
* **Commit(s) / branch** come from each cell's `summary.json` (`worktree @ commit`, node binary md5 in brackets), not from the dir name.
* **What a cell dir holds.** A matched-bench cell (`ozarchy-<tag>-<cell>`) has `summary.json`, `run.log`, `bench.log`,
  `agreement.jsonl`, `analysis-val*.txt`, `digest-*`, `drain*`, `funnel-val*.csv`, `phase-val*.csv`, `metrics-{before,after}-val*.txt`,
  `cpu.csv`, `schedstat.*`, `state-digest-val*.txt`, `val*.log.excerpt` (small, keep) and the bulky `val*.log.gz` (raw node logs),
  `buckets.csv`, `tasks.txt`, `sampler.csv`; profiled cells add `perf.data`, `perf.folded`, `prof-*`; liquidation / ADL cells add
  `liq-stress.json`, `liq-lines-val*.txt`, `thin-snap.jsonl`; 300-market cells add `genesis-3val.json`. `*-tools` dirs hold analysis scripts and
  derived perf dumps; `*-build` dirs hold build logs and binary md5s; `ubench-*` dirs hold microbench logs, perf data and staged binaries.
* **Recorded in** = where the results are written up. Section names are copied from the doc headings. Almost everything is in
  `docs/perf/ozarchy-antispam-item6-pf1-2026-10-04.md` (the long ozarchy doc, "the ozarchy doc" below). Per-cell numbers for every cell with a `summary.json` are in `cells.md`.
* **Status codes:** R = recorded in a doc; P = partly recorded, the extra file adds the rest; N = not recorded, the extra file is the record; S = skipped.
* **Deletable columns** are the Tier A (profiler output, staged binaries, regenerable genesis) and Tier B (bulky raw logs and sampler dumps) bytes from `DELETABLE.md`.

New summary files in this directory (campaigns the docs do not fully cover):

* `ozarchy-4acdc59-300m-void-runs.md`
* `ozarchy-antispam-rounds-cell-map.md`
* `ozarchy-harness-baseline-cap-ab.md`
* `ozarchy-main-300m-hostcheck.md`
* `read-precompile-gas.md`

Other files here: `cells.md` (every cell's numbers), `DELETABLE.md` (what can be removed from ozarchy), `tools/` (copies of the drivers, analysis scripts and patches that were not in git, same paths as on ozarchy; s29).
